- 27 library crates use only `thiserror` 
- 11 library crates use both `anyhow` + `thiserror` (valid — `anyhow` for internal convenience, `thiserror` at API boundary)
- 3 app/binary crates use `anyhow` (correct per CLAUDE.md)

### Plan

For each of the 8 crates, the fix is:

1. Add `thiserror` dependency to the crate's `Cargo.toml`
2. Create a `thiserror`-based `Error` enum covering the crate's failure modes (IO, database, config, HTTP, etc.)
3. Implement `From` conversions for upstream error types
4. Replace `anyhow::Result` in the public API with the crate's own `Result<T, Error>`
5. Keep `anyhow` internally only if genuinely needed for ad-hoc context; otherwise remove it

**Rough effort per crate:** small for thin crates like `caldav` or `qmd`, moderate for `memory` and `auth` which have more surface area.

## All Uses of `anyhow::Result`, `anyhow::bail!`, `anyhow::anyhow!`

### Error Categories Found

**1. HTTP/Connection Errors:**
- Invalid URL parsing (malformed CalDAV URL)
- TLS root loading failures
- Service discovery failures
- Principal/calendar home set lookup failures
- HTTP request failures (fetch, create, update, delete)

**2. Parsing Errors:**
- iCalendar format validation failures
- Event data parsing errors

**3. Configuration/Validation Errors:**
- Missing required parameters (operation, calendar, event_href, etag, summary, start)
- Missing account configuration
- Unknown CalDAV account name
- Unknown operation type
- Multiple accounts configured (ambiguous selection)
- Missing credentials (username, password)
- No URL and provider requiring one
- Missing credentials in account config

**4. Resource/Data Errors:**
- Event not found at given href
- Server HTTP error responses

## Summary of mcp-agent-bridge Crate

### 1. Dependencies (from Cargo.toml)

**Core Dependencies:**
- `anyhow` - Error handling (workspace)
- `async-trait` - Async trait support (workspace)
- `moltis-agents` - Agent tool registry and types (workspace)
- `moltis-mcp` - MCP protocol and tool bridge implementation (workspace)
- `serde_json` - JSON serialization (workspace)
- `tokio` - Async runtime (workspace)

**Optional Features:**
- `moltis-metrics` - Metrics collection (optional)
- `tracing` - Structured logging (optional, gated with `#[cfg(feature = "...")]`)

### 2. Public API (from lib.rs)

The crate exports exactly 2 items:
- `McpToolAdapter` - Struct implementing `AgentTool` by delegating to `McpToolBridge`
- `sync_mcp_tools` - Async function to synchronize MCP tools into the registry

### 3. All Uses of Anyhow Error Handling

The crate is **minimal and disciplined** in error handling:

**anyhow::Result usage:**
- `adapter.rs:37` - `McpToolAdapter::execute(&self, params: serde_json::Value) -> anyhow::Result<serde_json::Value>`
- `adapter.rs:93` - Test helper `FakeTool::execute` returns `anyhow::Result<serde_json::Value>`

**anyhow::Error conversion:**
- `adapter.rs:40` - `.map_err(anyhow::Error::from)` - converts `moltis_mcp::error::Error` to `anyhow::Error`

**No uses of:**
- `anyhow::bail!` - Not used
- `anyhow::anyhow!` - Not used
- `anyhow::Context` - Not used

### 4. Error Categories

The crate itself defines **zero error types**. All errors flow through:

1. **MCP errors** (`moltis_mcp::error::Error`) - from `McpToolBridge::execute()`:
   - `std::io::Error` (I/O failures)
   - `reqwest::Error` (HTTP failures)
   - `serde_json::Error` (JSON serialization)
   - `url::ParseError` (URL parsing)
   - `McpTransportError` (MCP transport protocol)
   - `McpManagerError` (MCP manager state)
   - `Message { message: String }` (Custom message errors)
   - `External { context, source }` (Wrapped external errors)

2. **Conversion pattern**: `mcp::error::Error` → `anyhow::Error` via `.map_err(anyhow::Error::from)` in the `execute()` method

### 5. All Public Function Signatures Returning anyhow::Result

| Function | Location | Signature |
|----------|----------|-----------|
| `McpToolAdapter::execute` | adapter.rs:37 | `async fn execute(&self, params: serde_json::Value) -> anyhow::Result<serde_json::Value>` |

**Note:** The `sync_mcp_tools` function does NOT return a Result:
```rust
pub async fn sync_mcp_tools(
    manager: &moltis_mcp::McpManager,
    registry: &Arc<RwLock<ToolRegistry>>,
)
```
It performs synchronous registry operations with no fallible steps.

### Architecture Pattern

This crate implements the **thin adapter pattern**:
- `McpToolAdapter` wraps `McpToolBridge` from the `moltis-mcp` crate
- `AgentTool` trait (from `moltis-agents`) requires `anyhow::Result`
- `McpAgentTool` trait (from `moltis-mcp`) uses its own `error::Result` type
- Error conversion happens at the boundary via `map_err(anyhow::Error::from)`

### 6. File Structure

Only 2 Rust files in the crate:
- `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/wiggly-hydrangea/crates/mcp-agent-bridge/src/lib.rs` - Module exports
- `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/wiggly-hydrangea/crates/mcp-agent-bridge/src/adapter.rs` - Core implementation + tests (72 lines public code, 88 lines tests)

All error handling uses **`anyhow::Result`** and **`anyhow::bail!`**. There are **NO uses of `anyhow::Context`** or custom error types.

### 4. Error Categories Identified

The crate has 9 distinct error categories, all operational/availability related:

1. **QMD Availability Errors** (4 instances)
   - "QMD is not available" - when binary not found or unavailable before operations

2. **Version Probe Errors** (1 instance)
   - "QMD version probe failed: {stderr}" - when version check command fails

3. **Timeout Errors** (1 instance)
   - "QMD command timed out after {ms}ms" - when subprocess exceeds timeout

4. **Collection Management Errors** (1 instance)
   - "QMD collection add failed for {name}: {stderr}" - when collection registration fails

5. **Index Update Errors** (1 instance)
   - "QMD update failed: {stderr}" - when index refresh fails

6. **Embedding Errors** (1 instance)
   - "QMD embed failed: {stderr}" - when embedding generation fails

7. **Search Errors** (1 instance)
   - "QMD search failed: {stderr}" - when search operation fails

8. **Document Retrieval Errors** (1 instance)
   - "QMD get failed: {stderr}" - when document fetch fails

9. **Memory Write + Reindex Composite Error** (1 instance)
   - "memory content was saved to {path} but QMD reindex failed: {error}" - combines two operations

### 5. **Error Categories**

The crate's errors fall into these categories:

1. **Configuration & Environment**
   - Cannot read config file
   - Cannot determine home directory
   - Cannot find moltis binary
   - Missing required arguments (command, binary)

2. **Protocol & Handshake**
   - Unexpected response ID
   - Handshake failed
   - Unexpected non-text message
   - WebSocket errors
   - Connection closed/timeout

3. **Platform Support**
   - Service operations not supported on current OS
   - Platform-specific launchd/systemd failures

4. **Command Execution**
   - Failed to execute command
   - Command timeout
   - Unsupported invoke command

5. **Service Management**
   - Service not installed
   - launchctl operations failed (bootstrap, kill, kickstart)
   - systemctl operations failed
   - OS service operations failed

### 6. **Error Handling Patterns**

- **? operator** - Direct propagation for fallible operations (most common)
- **map_err()** - Custom context wrapping for specific errors (file I/O, binary lookup)
- **ok_or_else()** - Converting `Option` to `Result` with custom error messages
- **anyhow::bail!()** - Immediate error return with formatted message
- **anyhow::anyhow!()** - Error construction (usually as Err variant)

### 7. **No anyhow::Context Uses**

The crate does not use `.context()` or `.with_context()` - all error augmentation is done via `map_err()` or `anyhow::anyhow!()` macros directly.

### 5. Error Categories by Domain

**I. SSH/Key Management (ssh_routes.rs)**
- SSH key generation failures (ssh-keygen exit codes)
- SSH key import/decryption failures (passphrase validation)
- Host key scanning failures (ssh-keyscan)
- Known-hosts format validation
- Passphrase protection detection
- File I/O (tempfile creation, reading/writing keys)
- UTF-8 conversion errors from command outputs
- Error handling uses:
  - `anyhow::bail!()` — for fatal errors with custom messages
  - `anyhow::anyhow!()` — for wrapping errors from external functions

**II. Gateway Initialization (server/runtime.rs, server/gateway.rs)**
- TLS certificate configuration (auto-generation vs user-provided)
- TLS certificate path validation
- Bind address parsing (`ip.parse()`)
- Ngrok tunnel initialization
- Background task spawning (cron, metrics, session events)
- File system operations (reading CA certs, config files)
- Error handling:
  - `?` operator for propagation
  - `anyhow::bail!()` for configuration errors (missing/invalid TLS setup)
  - `map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(...))` for wrapping lower-level errors

**III. ngrok Integration (server/ngrok.rs, server/runtime.rs)**
- ngrok session creation
- ngrok endpoint configuration
- ngrok tunnel startup/shutdown
- Forward target validation (`http://127.0.0.1:PORT`)
- URL parsing
- Error handling:
  - `anyhow::anyhow!()` for wrapping ngrok SDK errors
  - `?` for early return on failures

**IV. WebSocket Protocol (ws.rs)**
- Connection state during handshake
- Protocol version mismatch
- Frame parsing errors
- Message deserialization
- Error handling:
  - `anyhow::bail!()` for protocol violations
  - `?` for JSON parsing failures
  - Errors are never fatal to the connection — all errors in handshake trigger graceful shutdown

### 6. Error Usage Patterns

**Pattern 1: Command Execution (subprocess errors)**
```rust
let output = Command::new("ssh-keygen").output().await?;
if !output.status.success() {
    anyhow::bail!("{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).trim());
}
```

**Pattern 2: Configuration Validation**
```rust
let ip: std::net::IpAddr = bind
    .parse()
    .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("invalid bind address '{bind}': {e}"))?;
```

**Pattern 3: Resource Availability**
```rust
let app = stored.clone().ok_or_else(|| {
    anyhow::anyhow!("ngrok tunnel cannot start before the HTTP app is ready")
})?
```

**Pattern 4: Wrapping External Errors**
```rust
.map_err(|error| anyhow::anyhow!("invalid ngrok forward target: {error}"))?
```

**Pattern 5: Converting Result Types in HTTP Handlers**
```rust
// In ssh_routes.rs:
.map_err(|err| ApiError::internal(SSH_KEY_GENERATE_FAILED, err))?
```
(note: The SSH routes use a custom `ApiError` type that wraps the underlying error for HTTP responses)

### 7. Security Considerations

**Secrets Handling:**
- `SecretString` is used for SSH private keys and passphrases
- `expose_secret()` called only at necessary boundaries (file writes, subprocess arguments)

**SSRF/Binding Validation:**
- IP address parsing validates bind addresses before socket binding
- ngrok forward targets validated via URL parsing

**No `.unwrap()` in error paths** — all error handling uses `?` or explicit error mapping

### 8. File Size and Architecture

**Total files with anyhow::Result: 5 files**

**Largest files:**
- `server/runtime.rs` — ~1,400+ lines (gateway finalization and task spawning)
- `ssh_routes.rs` — ~1,100+ lines (SSH management API)
- `server/gateway.rs` — ~880+ lines (gateway preparation with webhooks)
- `ws.rs` — ~700+ lines (WebSocket handler)
- `server/ngrok.rs` — ~160 lines (ngrok controller)

All within or near the 1,500-line file size limit mentioned in CLAUDE.md.

### 9. Error Flow Summary

```
HTTP Request
  ↓
axum handler (returns Result<Response, ApiError> or anyhow::Result)
  ↓
Errors are either:
  a) Mapped to ApiError (HTTP routes)
  b) Converted to Response via IntoResponse trait
  c) Propagated via ? operator
  ↓
If anyhow::Result:
  - SSH key operations return application-level errors
  - Gateway startup returns initialization errors
  - ngrok operations return tunnel errors
  - WebSocket handshake returns protocol errors
```

## Summary: moltis-auth Crate Error Handling Analysis

### 1. Dependencies (from Cargo.toml)

**Key error-related dependencies:**
- `anyhow` (workspace) - Application error handling
- `thiserror` - NOT used in this crate
- `sqlx` - Database layer (sqlx errors propagate via `?`)
- `webauthn-rs` - WebAuthn errors propagate via `?`
- `secrecy` - Secret types for sensitive data

**Other relevant dependencies:**
- `argon2` - Password hashing (errors wrapped in anyhow)
- `openssl` - WebAuthn support

### 2. Public API (lib.rs)

**Public modules exported:**
- `credential_store` - Main auth store (passwords, passkeys, API keys, sessions, SSH keys)
- `locality` - Connection locality detection
- `webauthn` - WebAuthn (passkey) state management
- `vault` feature - Optional encryption at rest

**Public re-exports:**
- `credential_store::*` (all types and main CredentialStore)
- `locality::{has_proxy_headers, is_local_connection}`
- `webauthn::{WebAuthnRegistry, WebAuthnState, load_passkeys}`

---

### 3. All Uses of anyhow:: Across Files

**File-by-file anyhow usage count:**
- `sessions.rs` - 23 uses (password/session management)
- `ssh.rs` - 22 uses (SSH key/target management)
- `webauthn.rs` - 17 uses (passkey registration/authentication)
- `env_vars.rs` - 4 uses (environment variable storage)
- `api_keys.rs` - 4 uses (API key management)
- `types.rs` - 2 uses (SSH auth mode parsing)
- `util.rs` - 2 uses (crypto utilities)
- `credential_store.rs` - 0 (module definition only)
- `legacy.rs` - 0 (no error handling)
- `locality.rs` - 0 (pure logic, no Result type)
- `lib.rs` - 0 (module exports only)
- `tests.rs` - 0 (test code with allow directives)

**Total: 80 anyhow:: usages**

---

### 4. Error Handling Patterns

All functions returning `anyhow::Result<T>` follow one of these patterns:

#### Pattern 1: Propagate with `?` (Database/IO errors)
```rust
sqlx::query(...).execute(&self.pool).await?
// Propagates sqlx errors automatically
```

#### Pattern 2: Convert via `map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("context: {e}"))`
```rust
.map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("failed to hash password: {e}"))?
.map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("webauthn builder error: {e}"))?
```

#### Pattern 3: Explicit validation with `anyhow::bail!("message")`
```rust
anyhow::bail!("password already set")
anyhow::bail!("ssh key name is required")
anyhow::bail!("registration challenge expired")
```

#### Pattern 4: Option to error with `ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("message"))`
```rust
.ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("no pending registration for this challenge"))?
.ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("no pending authentication for this challenge"))?
```

**No `Context` trait usage found** - All error messages explicitly constructed.

---

### 5. Error Categories

**Database/Storage Errors** (propagated via `?`):
- SQLite connection failures
- Query execution errors
- Transaction failures
- Row count mismatches

**Cryptographic Errors**:
- Password hashing failures (`anyhow::anyhow!("failed to hash password: {e}")`)
- Vault encryption/decryption (when vault feature enabled)

**Validation Errors** (`anyhow::bail!`):
- Password already set / duplicate password attempt
- Missing required fields (SSH key name, target label, target address)
- Invalid enum values (`SshAuthMode::parse_db` → `"unknown ssh auth mode"`)
- Missing credentials for setup (`cannot mark setup complete without any credentials`)
- Incorrect password verification

**WebAuthn Challenge Errors**:
- No pending registration/authentication challenge found
- Challenge TTL expired (5 minute timeout)
- WebAuthn builder/library failures
- No passkeys registered for authentication
- Passkey registration/authentication completion failures

**SSH Management Errors**:
- SSH key still in use (cannot delete)
- Missing SSH private key in vault
- Invalid SSH auth mode
- SSH target not found

**Permission/Access Errors**:
- Invalid API key / no matching key in verification
- Vault not available for encrypted SSH key

---

## Summary: Comprehensive Analysis of `crates/skills/` Crate

### 1. Cargo.toml Dependencies

Dependencies from Cargo.toml:
- `anyhow` - Error handling (workspace = true)
- `async-trait` - Async trait support
- `flate2` - Gzip compression
- `moltis-config` - Configuration library
- `moltis-metrics` - Optional metrics
- `notify-debouncer-full` - Optional file watcher
- `reqwest` - HTTP client
- `serde/serde_json/serde_yaml` - Serialization
- `tar` - Tar archive handling
- `tokio` - Async runtime
- `tracing` - Logging
- `walkdir` - Directory traversal

### 2. Public API (lib.rs)

Module structure:
- `discover` - Skill discovery from filesystem
- `formats` - Plugin format adapters (SKILL.md, Claude Code, etc.)
- `install` - Repository installation and scanning
- `manifest` - Manifest storage and loading
- `migration` - Legacy plugins migration
- `parse` - SKILL.md parsing
- `portability` - Export/import bundle functionality
- `prompt_gen` - System prompt generation
- `registry` - In-memory skill registry
- `requirements` - Binary dependency checking
- `safety` - Prompt injection pattern scanning
- `watcher` - Optional filesystem watching

Public constant:
- `SIDECAR_SUBDIRS: &[&str]` - Skill sidecar directories (references, templates, assets, scripts)

### 4. Error Categories

The skills crate uses **only `anyhow::Result`** for error handling. Error categories implicitly present:

1. **Parsing Errors** (parse.rs):
   - Invalid SKILL.md frontmatter
   - Missing YAML closing delimiters
   - Invalid skill names
   - Missing slug when name validation fails

2. **Filesystem Errors** (install.rs, portability.rs):
   - Failed to read/write files
   - Archive extraction issues
   - Path traversal attempts (security)
   - Symlink detection (security)
   - Directory creation failures

3. **Installation Errors** (install.rs):
   - Missing repo directory
   - Repository already exists
   - No skills found in repository
   - Archive entries escaping directories
   - Invalid archive paths

4. **Manifest/Config Errors** (manifest.rs, discovery, registry.rs):
   - Repo not found in manifest
   - Skill not found in registry
   - Skill directory missing
   - Cannot remove non-registry skills

5. **Network Errors** (install.rs):
   - Failed to fetch from GitHub API
   - HTTP request failures

6. **Package Management Errors** (requirements.rs):
   - Missing required formula/package/module fields
   - Unsupported install kinds (download)
   - Command execution failures

7. **Bundle Errors** (portability.rs):
   - Unsupported bundle version
   - Missing bundle manifest
   - Invalid paths in bundle
   - Unsafe path components
   - Symlink/hardlink entries

## Summary: Error Handling in `crates/memory/` Crate

Based on my thorough examination of all Rust files in the memory crate, here are my findings:

### 1. **Dependencies** (`Cargo.toml`)

The crate uses **`anyhow`** for error handling:
- `anyhow = { workspace = true }` (line 34)
- No `thiserror` is used

### 2. **Public API** (`lib.rs`)

All public modules are re-exported without additional error handling wrapping:
- `chunker`, `config`, `embeddings*`, `manager`, `reranking`, `runtime`, `schema`, `search`, `session_export`, `store`, `store_sqlite`, `tools`, `watcher`, `writer`

### 3. **Error Handling Patterns Found**

All error handling uses **`anyhow::Result<T>`** exclusively. Found **121 total occurrences** across 17 files.

### 4. **Error Categories** (by domain)

#### A. **Database/Storage Errors** (`store.rs`, `store_sqlite.rs`)
- SQLite query failures (from `sqlx::query_*` operations)
- Transaction errors (`tx.commit()`)
- Database connection issues

**Example functions:**
```rust
async fn upsert_file(&self, file: &FileRow) -> anyhow::Result<()>
async fn vector_search(&self, query_embedding: &[f32], limit: usize) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<SearchResult>>
async fn keyword_search(&self, query: &str, limit: usize) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<SearchResult>>
```

#### B. **Embedding Provider Errors** (`embeddings*.rs`)
- OpenAI API HTTP errors: `.error_for_status()?`
- Network errors from `reqwest::Client`
- Batch API failures with timeout: `anyhow::bail!("batch embedding timed out after 60 minutes")`
- Local GGUF model loading/tokenization failures: `anyhow::anyhow!("failed to load GGUF model: {e}")`

**Example functions:**
```rust
async fn embed(&self, text: &str) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<f32>>
async fn embed_batch(&self, texts: &[String]) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<Vec<f32>>>
async fn batch_embed(&self, texts: &[String]) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<Vec<f32>>>
```

#### C. **File I/O Errors** (`manager.rs`, `writer.rs`, `tools.rs`, `session_export.rs`)
- `tokio::fs::read_to_string()`
- `tokio::fs::write()`, `tokio::fs::rename()`
- `tokio::fs::remove_file()`, `tokio::fs::create_dir_all()`
- File metadata access: `tokio::fs::metadata()`

**Example validation with `anyhow::bail!`:**
```rust
fn validate_memory_path(data_dir: &Path, file: &str) -> anyhow::Result<PathBuf> {
    if path.is_empty() {
        anyhow::bail!("memory path cannot be empty");
    }
    if Path::new(path).is_absolute() {
        anyhow::bail!("memory path must be relative");
    }
    // ... more checks
}
```

#### D. **Content Validation & Transformation Errors** (`writer.rs`, `reranking.rs`)
- Memory path traversal checks: `anyhow::bail!("...")`
- Text removal validation: `anyhow::bail!("text to remove cannot be empty")`
- LLM response parsing: `serde_json::from_str(json_str)?`
- Score validation: `anyhow::bail!("expected {} scores, got {}", expected_count, scores.len())`

#### E. **Semantic/Configuration Errors** (`watcher.rs`, `embeddings_batch.rs`, `embeddings_fallback.rs`)
- Missing configuration: `anyhow::anyhow!("memory writes are disabled (no data_dir configured)")`
- Size limit violations: `anyhow::bail!("content exceeds maximum size...")`
- Provider chain exhaustion: `anyhow::bail!("all embedding providers failed: {}")`
- Batch API state errors: `anyhow::bail!("batch {} ended with status: {}")`

#### F. **Context-Based Error Enhancement** (`embeddings_local.rs`, `session_export.rs`)
Uses `.context()` from `anyhow::Context` trait for error enrichment:
```rust
tokio::fs::create_dir_all(&cache_dir)
    .await
    .context("creating model cache dir")?;
    
reqwest::get(DEFAULT_MODEL_URL)
    .await
    .context("downloading GGUF model")?
    .error_for_status()
    .context("GGUF model download failed")?;
```

### 5. **All Public Functions Returning `anyhow::Result`**

**Trait Methods (async):**
- `EmbeddingProvider` trait: `embed()`, `embed_batch()`
- `MemoryStore` trait: `upsert_file()`, `get_file()`, `delete_file()`, `list_files()`, `upsert_chunks()`, `get_chunks_for_file()`, `delete_chunks_for_file()`, `get_chunk_by_id()`, `get_cached_embedding()`, `put_cached_embedding()`, `put_cached_embeddings_batch()`, `count_cached_embeddings()`, `evict_embedding_cache()`, `vector_search()`, `keyword_search()`
- `MemoryRuntime` trait: `sync()`, `sync_path()`, `remove_path()`, `search()`, `get_chunk()`, `status()`
- `MemoryWriter` trait: `write_memory()`
- `RerankerProvider` trait: `rerank()`
- `LlmClient` trait: `complete()`
- `AgentTool` trait: `execute()` (for `MemorySearchTool`, `MemoryGetTool`, `MemorySaveTool`, `MemoryDeleteTool`)

**Manager Methods (async):**
- `MemoryManager::resolve_file_by_hash_prefix()`
- `MemoryManager::sync()`
- `MemoryManager::sync_path()`
- `MemoryManager::remove_path()`
- `MemoryManager::search()`
- `MemoryManager::get_chunk()`
- `MemoryManager::status()`
- `MemoryManager::write_memory()` (MemoryWriter impl)

**Helper Functions (async):**
- `validate_memory_path()` - path validation
- `remove_exact_text()` - text manipulation
- `LocalGgufEmbeddingProvider::new()` - GGUF model loading
- `LocalGgufEmbeddingProvider::ensure_model()` - model download
- `SessionExporter::export()` - session file export
- `SessionExporter::cleanup()` - cleanup old exports
- `MemoryFileWatcher::start()` - filesystem watcher initialization
- `LlmReranker::parse_scores()` - JSON parsing

**Batch API Functions (async):**
- `BatchEmbeddingProvider::batch_embed()`

**Fallback Chain Functions (async):**
- `FallbackEmbeddingProvider::embed()`
- `FallbackEmbeddingProvider::embed_batch()`

**Search Functions (async):**
- `hybrid_search()` - vector + keyword merge
- `keyword_only_search()` (inferred from manager.rs)

**Contract Test Functions (async):**
- `ingest_then_search_returns_result()`
- `delete_removes_from_search()`
- `keyword_search_finds_exact_match()`
- `empty_search_returns_empty()`

**Schema Functions (async):**
- `run_migrations()` - SQLx migrations

### 6. **Error Composition Strategy**

1. **Propagation via `?` operator**: All fallible async operations use `?` to propagate errors up the call stack
2. **Explicit error creation via `anyhow::bail!()`**: For validation failures and user-facing errors
3. **Error wrapping via `anyhow::anyhow!()`**: For lower-level library errors (e.g., HTTP, GGUF loading)
4. **Context enrichment via `.context()`**: For adding diagnostic information to errors from external crates
5. **Silent fallback on non-fatal errors**: Circuit breaker in `FallbackEmbeddingProvider` logs warnings but continues

### 7. **File-by-File Error Type Summary**

| File | Main Error Sources |
|------|-------------------|
| `store.rs` | Trait definition (no impl) |
| `store_sqlite.rs` | SQLx query failures, type conversions |
| `manager.rs` | File I/O, SHA256 hashing, store operations, embeddings |
| `embeddings.rs` | Trait definition (no impl) |
| `embeddings_openai.rs` | HTTP requests, JSON parsing |
| `embeddings_batch.rs` | Batch API HTTP, JSONL parsing, timeout logic |
| `embeddings_fallback.rs` | Provider exhaustion (custom bail) |
| `embeddings_local.rs` | GGUF model loading, tokenization, FFI errors with `.context()` |
| `writer.rs` | Path validation (bail), text manipulation |
| `tools.rs` | File system operations, checkpoints, parameter validation |
| `search.rs` | Embedding generation, store queries |
| `reranking.rs` | LLM API calls, JSON parsing, score validation |
| `runtime.rs` | Trait delegation (no new errors) |
| `schema.rs` | SQLx migrations |
| `session_export.rs` | File operations, with `.context()` enrichment |
| `watcher.rs` | File watcher setup, event handling |
| `config.rs` | No error handling (data structures only) |
| `chunker.rs` | No error handling (deterministic parsing) |
| `contract.rs` | Test assertions (no errors) |

All functions follow the CLAUDE.md guideline: **`anyhow::Result` for app errors**, with clear error messages at user-facing boundaries (tools, writer, validation) and lower-level propagation elsewhere.

Now I have the full picture. Key constraints:

- `AgentTool::execute` (in `moltis-agents`) returns `anyhow::Result` — forces `mcp-agent-bridge`
- `MemoryWriter` trait (in `moltis-agents`) returns `anyhow::Result` — forces `memory` and `qmd`  
- `MemoryRuntime` trait (in `memory`) returns `anyhow::Result` — forces `qmd`

Strategy: Each crate gets its own `thiserror` Error type. Own public APIs use it. Where external trait boundaries force `anyhow::Result`, the typed error converts automatically via `?` (thiserror auto-derives `std::error::Error`).

Starting with the 4 simpler crates in parallel:

Read all .rs source files in the crate first to understand the code.\n\n2. Add `thiserror = { workspace = true }` to `Cargo.toml` (keep `anyhow` for now — we'll use it only internally if needed).\n\n3. Create `src/error.rs` with a `thiserror` Error enum. Based on the error patterns found:\n   - HTTP/connection errors (from reqwest, hyper)\n   - CalDAV protocol/discovery errors (from libdav)\n   - iCalendar parsing errors  \n   - Configuration/validation errors (missing fields, unknown accounts)\n   - Resource errors (event not found)\n\n   Example structure:\n   ```rust\n   use thiserror::Error;\n   \n   #[derive(Debug, Error)]\n   pub enum Error {\n       #[error(\"invalid CalDAV URL: {0}\")]\n       InvalidUrl(#[from] url::ParseError),\n       \n       #[error(\"{0}\")]\n       Http(#[from] reqwest::Error),\n       // etc.\n   }\n   \n   pub type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, Error>;\n   ```\n\n4. Update `lib.rs` to add `pub mod error;` and re-export: `pub use error::Error;`\n\n5. Update the `CalDavClient` trait in `client.rs` to use `crate::error::Result<T>` instead of `anyhow::Result<T>`.\n\n6. Update all implementations (`LibDavCalDavClient`, mock in tests) to use the crate's `Result`.\n\n7. Update `ical.rs` public functions to use the crate's `Result`.\n\n8. Update `tool.rs` — the `execute` method returns `anyhow::Result` because it implements `AgentTool`, so that stays `anyhow::Result`. But internal helper methods like `resolve_client` should use the crate's `Result`.\n\n8. Replace `anyhow::bail!(\"message\")` with returning the appropriate Error variant. Replace `map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"context: {e}\"))` with proper `From` impls or map to Error variants.\n\n9. Try to remove the `anyhow` dependency from Cargo.toml entirely if possible. If `tool.rs` needs it for `AgentTool::execute`, keep it just for that.\n\n**Rules**:\n- Don't add `anyhow` to the error type itself (no `#[from] anyhow::Error`)\n- Use `#[from]` for automatic conversions where possible\n- Use `#[error(...)]` with descriptive messages\n- Keep the Error enum focused — don't over-engineer with too many variants\n- For string-based validation errors, a single `#[error(\"{0}\")] Validation(String)` variant is fine\n- Make sure all existing tests still compile\n\nWrite all the code changes. Do NOT just describe what to do — actually edit the files."

Here is a summary of what was done:

## Summary

Added a `thiserror`-based `Error` type to the `moltis-skills` crate and converted its entire public API from `anyhow::Result` to the crate's own `Result` alias. All changes are staged in git.

### Files changed (13 total)

**New file:**
- `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/wiggly-hydrangea/crates/skills/src/error.rs` -- `thiserror` Error enum with 11 variants: `Io`, `Json`, `Yaml`, `Http`, `Walk`, `Join` (all with `#[from]`), plus string-based `Parse`, `Install`, `NotFound`, `Validation`, `Bundle`, and a feature-gated `Notify` for `file-watcher`.

**Modified files:**
- `Cargo.toml` -- Replaced `anyhow` dependency with `thiserror`
- `lib.rs` -- Added `pub mod error;` and `pub use error::Error;`
- `discover.rs` -- `SkillDiscoverer` trait and `FsSkillDiscoverer` impl use `crate::error::Result`
- `formats.rs` -- `FormatAdapter` trait and `ClaudeCodeAdapter` methods use `crate::error::Result`
- `manifest.rs` -- `ManifestStore::default_path()`, `load()`, `save()` use `crate::error::Result`
- `parse.rs` -- `parse_metadata()`, `parse_skill()`, `split_frontmatter()` use `Result`; `bail!` replaced with `Error::Parse`/`Error::Validation`; `.context()` replaced with `.map_err()`/`.ok_or_else()`
- `install.rs` -- All functions use `Result`; `anyhow::bail!` replaced with `Error::Install`/`Error::Validation`/`Error::NotFound`/`Error::Parse`
- `portability.rs` -- All functions use `Result`; `bail!`/`.with_context()` replaced with `Error::Bundle`/`Error::Validation`/`Error::NotFound`/`Error::Install`
- `registry.rs` -- `SkillRegistry` trait and `InMemoryRegistry` impl use `crate::error::Result`
- `requirements.rs` -- `install_program_and_args()`, `run_install()` etc. use `Result`; `.context()` replaced with `.ok_or_else()`
- `migration.rs` -- `try_migrate()` and `copy_dir_recursive()` use `crate::error::Result`
- `watcher.rs` -- `default_watch_specs()` and `SkillWatcher::start()` use `crate::error::Result`

### Downstream compatibility

All downstream crates (`moltis-gateway`, `moltis-tools`, `moltis-agents`, `moltis-web`, `moltis-httpd`) compile without changes because `moltis_skills::Error` derives `std::error::Error` via `thiserror`, so the `?` operator auto-converts when used in `anyhow::Result` contexts.

commit cf22b22946746ee227f03e18de66fd4e1370508c
Author: Fabien Penso <gpg@pen.so>
Date:   Sun Apr 19 11:01:59 2026 +0100

    feat(auth): add thiserror-based Error type to moltis-auth crate
    
    Replace anyhow::Result with a crate-specific Error enum and Result
    type alias across the entire moltis-auth public API. This gives
    downstream crates structured error handling instead of opaque
    anyhow errors.
    
    Error variants: Database (sqlx), Io, Config, Json, Crypto,
    Validation, WebAuthn, and Ssh. The anyhow dependency is fully
    removed from the crate.

--- a/crates/code-index/src/config.rs

@@ -157,28 +157,22 @@ impl CodeIndexConfig {
         let path_lower = relative_path.to_ascii_lowercase();
         // Normalize separators.
         let path_forward = path_lower.replace('\\', "/");
-        self.skip_paths
-            .iter()
-            .any(|pattern| {
-                // Strip trailing slashes so "vendor/" is treated as "vendor" for
-                // segment matching.
-                let p = pattern.trim_end_matches('/').to_ascii_lowercase();
-                // Exact prefix match (handles both "vendor/foo" and "vendor/").
-                if path_forward.starts_with(&p)
-                    || path_forward.starts_with(&format!("{p}/"))
-                {
-                    return true;
-                }
-                // Segment match: check if any path component equals the pattern.
-                // This catches "src/vendor/foo.rs" when pattern is "vendor".
-                if !p.contains('/') {

-                        .split('/')
-                        .any(|segment| segment == p.as_str())
-                } else {
-                    false
-                }
-            })
+        self.skip_paths.iter().any(|pattern| {
+            // Strip trailing slashes so "vendor/" is treated as "vendor" for
+            // segment matching.
+            let p = pattern.trim_end_matches('/').to_ascii_lowercase();
+            // Exact prefix match (handles both "vendor/foo" and "vendor/").
+            if path_forward.starts_with(&p) || path_forward.starts_with(&format!("{p}/")) {
+                return true;
+            }
+            // Segment match: check if any path component equals the pattern.
+            // This catches "src/vendor/foo.rs" when pattern is "vendor".
+            if !p.contains('/') {
+                path_forward.split('/').any(|segment| segment == p.as_str())
+            } else {
+                false
+            }
+        })
     }
 
     /// Return a [`FilterConfig`](crate::filter::FilterConfig) for the file watcher.

//! Crate-level error type for `moltis-memory`.

use thiserror::Error;

#[derive(Debug, Error)]
pub enum Error {
    #[error(transparent)]
    Database(#[from] sqlx::Error),

    #[error(transparent)]
    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),

    #[error(transparent)]
    Json(#[from] serde_json::Error),

    #[error(transparent)]
    Http(#[from] reqwest::Error),

    #[error(transparent)]
    Migration(#[from] sqlx::migrate::MigrateError),

    #[error("{0}")]
    Embedding(String),

    #[error("{0}")]
    Validation(String),

    #[error("{0}")]
    Reranking(String),
}

pub type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, Error>;
```

---

**Changes made:**

1. **`crates/qmd/Cargo.toml`** -- Added `thiserror = { workspace = true }` dependency. Kept `anyhow` since `runtime.rs` needs it for trait impls.

2. **`crates/qmd/src/error.rs`** (new file) -- Defined `Error` enum with five variants:
   - `NotAvailable` -- QMD binary not found/available
   - `Timeout(u64)` -- command timed out
   - `CommandFailed { command, stderr }` -- non-zero exit code
   - `Io` (from `std::io::Error`) -- I/O failures
   - `Json` (from `serde_json::Error`) -- JSON parse failures
   - `Utf8` (from `std::string::FromUtf8Error`) -- UTF-8 decode failures (used by `get_document`)
   
   Also defines `pub type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, Error>`.

3. **`crates/qmd/src/lib.rs`** -- Added `pub mod error;` and `pub use error::Error;`.

4. **`crates/qmd/src/manager.rs`** -- Converted all public methods (`version`, `ensure_collection`, `ensure_collections`, `refresh_index`, `search`, `keyword_search`, `vector_search`, `hybrid_search`, `get_document`) and private helpers (`run_with_timeout`, `collection_exists`) from `anyhow::Result` to `crate::error::Result`. Replaced all `anyhow::bail!` calls with the appropriate `Error` variants.

5. **`crates/qmd/src/runtime.rs`** -- No changes needed. The `MemoryRuntime` and `MemoryWriter` trait methods return `anyhow::Result`. The crate's `Error` implements `std::error::Error` (via `thiserror`), so `?` auto-converts `crate::error::Error` to `anyhow::Error` in the trait impl methods.

Here is a summary of all changes made:

## Summary

### New file: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/wiggly-hydrangea/crates/skills/src/error.rs`

Created a `thiserror`-based error type with these variants:
- `Io` (from `std::io::Error`)
- `Json` (from `serde_json::Error`)
- `Yaml` (from `serde_yaml::Error`)
- `Http` (from `reqwest::Error`)
- `Walk` (from `walkdir::Error`)
- `Join` (from `tokio::task::JoinError`)
- `Notify` (from `notify_debouncer_full::notify::Error`, feature-gated behind `file-watcher`)
- `Parse(String)` -- for parsing failures
- `Install(String)` -- for installation failures
- `NotFound(String)` -- for missing resources
- `Validation(String)` -- for validation errors
- `Bundle(String)` -- for portability/bundle errors

Plus `pub type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, Error>;`

### Modified files (12 total):

1. **`Cargo.toml`** -- Replaced `anyhow` with `thiserror` dependency
2. **`lib.rs`** -- Added `pub mod error;` and `pub use error::Error;`
3. **`discover.rs`** -- `SkillDiscoverer` trait and `FsSkillDiscoverer` impl now use `crate::error::Result`
4. **`formats.rs`** -- `FormatAdapter` trait and `ClaudeCodeAdapter` impl now use `crate::error::Result`; `scan_with_adapter` returns `Option<Result<...>>`
5. **`install.rs`** -- All public functions (`install_skill`, `remove_repo`, `scan_repo_skills`, `default_install_dir`) and private helpers converted; `anyhow::bail!` replaced with `return Err(Error::Install(...))` / `Error::Parse(...)` / `Error::NotFound(...)`
6. **`manifest.rs`** -- `default_path()`, `load()`, `save()` use crate `Result`
7. **`migration.rs`** -- `try_migrate()` and `copy_dir_recursive()` use crate `Result`
8. **`parse.rs`** -- All public parsing functions use crate `Result`; `bail!` replaced with `Error::Validation(...)` / `Error::Parse(...)`; `.context()` replaced with `.map_err()` / `.ok_or_else()`
9. **`portability.rs`** -- All export/import functions use crate `Result`; `bail!` replaced with `Error::Bundle(...)` / `Error::NotFound(...)`; `.with_context()` replaced with `.map_err()`
10. **`registry.rs`** -- `SkillRegistry` trait and `InMemoryRegistry` impl use crate `Result`; `anyhow::anyhow!` / `anyhow::bail!` replaced with `Error::NotFound(...)` / `Error::Install(...)` / `Error::Validation(...)`
11. **`requirements.rs`** -- All install/preview functions use crate `Result`; `.context()` replaced with `.ok_or_else()`
12. **`watcher.rs`** -- Uses `crate::error::Result` instead of `anyhow::Result`

### Compilation result:
- `cargo check -p moltis-skills` -- **passes**
- `cargo check -p moltis-tools` -- **passes** (direct downstream dependent)
- `cargo test -p moltis-skills` -- **all 112 tests pass**
- `cargo check -p moltis-gateway` -- fails due to pre-existing unrelated error in `moltis-qmd` (which has its own incomplete `anyhow->thiserror` conversion in uncommitted changes on this branch)

The `moltis_skills::Error` type implements `std::error::Error` (via `thiserror`), so downstream crates using `anyhow::Result` can convert it automatically via `?`.

3. **`src/error.rs`** - Already existed with correct content (unchanged).

7. **`src/runtime.rs`** - Changed `MemoryRuntime` trait methods (`sync`, `sync_path`, `remove_path`, `search`, `get_chunk`, `status`) and the `MemoryManager` impl from `anyhow::Result` to `crate::error::Result`.

8. **`src/store_sqlite.rs`** - Changed all `MemoryStore` impl methods from `anyhow::Result` to `crate::error::Result`.

9. **`src/schema.rs`** - Changed `run_migrations` from `anyhow::Result` to `crate::error::Result`.

10. **`src/search.rs`** - Changed `hybrid_search` and `keyword_only_search` from `anyhow::Result` to `crate::error::Result`.

11. **`src/manager.rs`** - Changed public methods (`resolve_file_by_hash_prefix`, `sync`, `sync_path`, `remove_path`, `sync_file`, `search`, `get_chunk`, `status`) from `anyhow::Result` to `crate::error::Result`. Kept `MemoryWriter::write_memory` as `anyhow::Result` (trait defined in `moltis-agents`). Updated test mock `EmbeddingProvider` impls.

12. **`src/embeddings_openai.rs`** - Changed `embed` and `embed_batch` trait impls to `crate::error::Result`. Replaced `anyhow::anyhow!` with `Error::Embedding(...)`.

13. **`src/embeddings_batch.rs`** - Changed `use anyhow::Result` to `use crate::error::{Error, Result}`. Replaced all `anyhow::bail!` and `anyhow::anyhow!` with `Error::Embedding(...)`.

14. **`src/embeddings_fallback.rs`** - Changed `embed` and `embed_batch` trait impls to `crate::error::Result`. Replaced `anyhow::bail!` with `Err(Error::Embedding(...))`. Updated test mock impls.

15. **`src/embeddings_local.rs`** - Changed `embed` trait impl to `crate::error::Result`. Added `.map_err(|e| Error::Embedding(e.to_string()))` for the internal `anyhow::Result` conversion. Kept `anyhow` for non-trait internal FFI functions.

16. **`src/session_export.rs`** - Changed `export` and `cleanup` methods to `crate::error::Result`.

17. **`src/writer.rs`** - Changed `validate_memory_path` and `remove_exact_text` to `crate::error::Result`. Replaced all `anyhow::bail!` with `return Err(Error::Validation(...))`.

18. **`src/tools.rs`** - Added `Ok(...)` wrapper around `validate_memory_path` call to convert `crate::error::Result` to `anyhow::Result`. Updated test mock `EmbeddingProvider` impls. Kept `AgentTool::execute` methods as `anyhow::Result`.

**Files modified outside `crates/memory/`:**

19. **`crates/qmd/src/runtime.rs`** - Updated `MemoryRuntime` impl for `QmdMemoryRuntime` to return `MemoryResult` (aliased from `moltis_memory::Result`). Added `.map_err(|e| moltis_memory::Error::Validation(e.to_string()))` for QMD-specific `anyhow::Result` calls. Fixed `refresh_qmd_index` to properly convert the qmd crate's own error type.

20. **`crates/chat/src/memory_tools/tests.rs`** - Updated test mock `EmbeddingProvider::embed` to return `moltis_memory::Result`.

**Verification:** `cargo check -p moltis-memory -p moltis-qmd -p moltis-chat` succeeds with zero warnings and zero errors.

Everything is clean. 367 tests pass, workspace compiles, formatting passes. Here's the final summary:

## Summary

**8 library crates** converted from `anyhow` to `thiserror`-based error types:

| Crate | Error variants | `anyhow` removed? |
|-------|---------------|-------------------|
| `moltis-auth` | `Database`, `Io`, `Config`, `Json`, `Crypto`, `Validation`, `WebAuthn`, `Ssh` | Yes |
| `moltis-caldav` | `Io`, `InvalidUrl`, `Protocol`, `IcalParse`, `Validation`, `NotFound` | No (AgentTool) |
| `moltis-httpd` | `Io`, `Config`, `Tls`, `Ssh`, `Protocol`, `Ngrok`, `Other` | No (internal) |
| `moltis-mcp-agent-bridge` | `Mcp` | No (AgentTool) |
| `moltis-memory` | `Database`, `Io`, `Json`, `Http`, `Migration`, `Embedding`, `Validation`, `Reranking` | No (MemoryWriter/AgentTool) |
| `moltis-node-host` | `Io`, `Json`, `Url`, `WebSocket`, `Config`, `Protocol`, `Service`, `Command`, `UnsupportedPlatform` | Yes |
| `moltis-qmd` | `NotAvailable`, `Timeout`, `CommandFailed`, `Io`, `Json`, `Utf8` | No (MemoryRuntime) |
| `moltis-skills` | `Io`, `Json`, `Yaml`, `Http`, `Walk`, `Join`, `Notify`, `Parse`, `Install`, `NotFound`, `Validation`, `Bundle` | Yes |

**Downstream fixes** (6 files): `cli/main.rs`, `cli/node_commands.rs`, `gateway/helpers.rs`, `gateway/init_code_index.rs`, `code-index/index.rs`, `tools/skill_tools/read.rs`, `chat/memory_tools/tests.rs` — all updated to use the new error types at trait/call boundaries.

**70 files changed**, 732 insertions, 568 deletions. Some `code-index` formatting changes were pulled in by `cargo fmt`.

---
File: crates/httpd/src/error.rs:24
Body: <a href="#"><img alt="P2" src="https://greptile-static-assets.s3.amazonaws.com/badges/p2.svg?v=7" align="top"></a> **`Other` escape hatch undermines typed errors**

`Other(#[from] Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>)` is a catch-all that allows any unclassified error to bypass the typed variant system — any `?` on an unrecognised error type silently becomes `Other`. Since the entire goal of this PR is to expose typed errors to callers, `Other` makes the public API's error handling equivalent to `anyhow` for those cases.

Consider either eliminating `Other` and mapping each concrete error site explicitly (audit `runtime.rs`/`gateway.rs`), or at minimum documenting which errors are intentionally left unclassified here.

---
File: crates/mcp-agent-bridge/src/error.rs:9
Body: <a href="#"><img alt="P2" src="https://greptile-static-assets.s3.amazonaws.com/badges/p2.svg?v=7" align="top"></a> **Single-variant enum is an opaque newtype**

`Error` has only one variant (`Mcp`), making `mcp_agent_bridge::Error` a transparent wrapper over `moltis_mcp::error::Error`. Callers can't match on anything beyond "it's an MCP error". If no additional variants are expected short-term, consider using a type alias (`pub type Error = moltis_mcp::error::Error;`) or a `#[error(transparent)]` newtype struct instead of an enum, which signals that this is intentionally a passthrough rather than suggesting future variants.

Note: If this suggestion doesn't match your team's coding style, reply to this and let me know. I'll remember it for next time!
```

     1→//! Bridge between MCP tools and the agent tool registry.
     2→//!
     3→//! Adapts [`McpToolBridge`](moltis_mcp::tool_bridge::McpToolBridge) (mcp crate)
     4→//! to [`AgentTool`] (agents crate) and provides sync logic to register MCP tools
     5→//! into a [`ToolRegistry`].
     6→
     7→use std::sync::Arc;
     8→
     9→use {async_trait::async_trait, tokio::sync::RwLock};
    10→
    11→#[cfg(feature = "tracing")]
    12→use tracing::info;
    13→
    14→use {
    15→    moltis_agents::tool_registry::{AgentTool, ToolRegistry},
    16→    moltis_mcp::tool_bridge::{McpAgentTool, McpToolBridge},
    17→};
    18→
    19→/// Thin adapter that implements `AgentTool` (agents crate) by delegating to
    20→/// `McpToolBridge` which implements `McpAgentTool` (mcp crate).
    21→pub struct McpToolAdapter(pub McpToolBridge);
    22→
    23→#[async_trait]
    24→impl AgentTool for McpToolAdapter {
    25→    fn name(&self) -> &str {
    26→        McpAgentTool::name(&self.0)
    27→    }
    28→
    29→    fn description(&self) -> &str {
    30→        McpAgentTool::description(&self.0)
    31→    }
    32→
    33→    fn parameters_schema(&self) -> serde_json::Value {
    34→        McpAgentTool::parameters_schema(&self.0)
    35→    }
    36→
    37→    async fn execute(&self, params: serde_json::Value) -> anyhow::Result<serde_json::Value> {
    38→        Ok(McpAgentTool::execute(&self.0, params)
    39→            .await
    40→            .map_err(crate::error::Error::from)?)
    41→    }
    42→}
    43→
    44→/// Synchronize MCP tool bridges into the shared [`ToolRegistry`].
    45→///
    46→/// Removes all existing `mcp__*` tools and re-registers current bridges.
    47→#[cfg_attr(feature = "tracing", tracing::instrument(skip_all, fields(tool_count)))]
    48→pub async fn sync_mcp_tools(
    49→    manager: &moltis_mcp::McpManager,
    50→    registry: &Arc<RwLock<ToolRegistry>>,
    51→) {
    52→    let bridges = manager.tool_bridges().await;
    53→
    54→    let mut reg = registry.write().await;
    55→
    56→    // Remove all MCP-sourced tools before re-registering current ones.
    57→    reg.unregister_mcp();
    58→
    59→    // Register current bridges with their server name metadata.
    60→    let count = bridges.len();
    61→    for bridge in bridges {
    62→        let server = bridge.server_name().to_string();
    63→        reg.register_mcp(Box::new(McpToolAdapter(bridge)), server);
    64→    }
    65→
    66→    #[cfg(feature = "tracing")]
    67→    if count > 0 {
    68→        info!(tools = count, "MCP tools synced into tool registry");
    69→    }
    70→}
    71→
    72→#[cfg(test)]
    73→mod tests {
    74→    use {super::*, moltis_mcp::McpRegistry};
    75→
    76→    /// Minimal fake tool implementing AgentTool for testing sync logic.
    77→    struct FakeTool(&'static str);
    78→
    79→    #[async_trait]
    80→    impl AgentTool for FakeTool {
    81→        fn name(&self) -> &str {
    82→            self.0
    83→        }
    84→
    85→        fn description(&self) -> &str {
    86→            "fake"
    87→        }
    88→
    89→        fn parameters_schema(&self) -> serde_json::Value {
    90→            serde_json::json!({})
    91→        }
    92→
    93→        async fn execute(&self, _params: serde_json::Value) -> anyhow::Result<serde_json::Value> {
    94→            Ok(serde_json::json!({}))
    95→        }
    96→    }
    97→
    98→    #[tokio::test]
    99→    async fn test_sync_mcp_tools_empty_manager() {
   100→        let registry = Arc::new(RwLock::new(ToolRegistry::new()));
   101→        let reg = McpRegistry::load(&std::path::PathBuf::from("/nonexistent")).unwrap_or_default();
   102→        let manager = moltis_mcp::McpManager::new_with_env_overrides(
   103→            reg,
   104→            std::collections::HashMap::new(),
   105→            std::time::Duration::from_secs(30),
   106→        );
   107→
   108→        sync_mcp_tools(&manager, &registry).await;
   109→        let reg_guard = registry.read().await;
   110→        assert!(reg_guard.list_schemas().is_empty());
   111→    }
   112→
   113→    #[tokio::test]
   114→    async fn test_sync_mcp_tools_removes_stale_tools() {
   115→        let registry = Arc::new(RwLock::new(ToolRegistry::new()));
   116→        let reg = McpRegistry::load(&std::path::PathBuf::from("/nonexistent")).unwrap_or_default();
   117→        let manager = moltis_mcp::McpManager::new_with_env_overrides(
   118→            reg,
   119→            std::collections::HashMap::new(),
   120→            std::time::Duration::from_secs(30),
   121→        );
   122→
   123→        // Manually register an MCP tool, then sync (which should clear it).
   124→        {
   125→            let mut reg_guard = registry.write().await;
   126→            reg_guard.register_mcp(Box::new(FakeTool("stale_tool")), "stale_server".to_string());
   127→        }
   128→
   129→        sync_mcp_tools(&manager, &registry).await;
   130→        let reg_guard = registry.read().await;
   131→        assert!(
   132→            reg_guard.list_schemas().is_empty(),
   133→            "stale MCP tools should be removed"
   134→        );
   135→    }
   136→
   137→    #[tokio::test]
   138→    async fn test_sync_preserves_non_mcp_tools() {
   139→        let registry = Arc::new(RwLock::new(ToolRegistry::new()));
   140→        let reg = McpRegistry::load(&std::path::PathBuf::from("/nonexistent")).unwrap_or_default();
   141→        let manager = moltis_mcp::McpManager::new_with_env_overrides(
   142→            reg,
   143→            std::collections::HashMap::new(),
   144→            std::time::Duration::from_secs(30),
   145→        );
   146→
   147→        // Register a non-MCP tool before sync — it should survive.
   148→        {
   149→            let mut reg_guard = registry.write().await;
   150→            reg_guard.register(Box::new(FakeTool("builtin_tool")));
   151→        }
   152→
   153→        sync_mcp_tools(&manager, &registry).await;
   154→        let reg_guard = registry.read().await;
   155→        let schemas = reg_guard.list_schemas();
   156→        assert_eq!(schemas.len(), 1, "non-MCP tool should survive sync");
   157→        assert_eq!(schemas[0]["name"], "builtin_tool");
   158→    }
   159→}

   352→
   353→    /// Search using the specified mode.
   354→    pub async fn search(
   355→        &self,
   356→        query: &str,
   357→        mode: SearchMode,
   358→        limit: usize,
   359→    ) -> Result<Vec<QmdSearchResult>> {
   360→        if !self.is_available().await {
   361→            return Err(Error::NotAvailable);
   362→        }
   363→
   364→        let mut command = self.command_with_index();
   365→        let effective_limit = if limit == 0 {
   366→            self.config.max_results.max(1)
   367→        } else {
   368→            limit.min(self.config.max_results.max(1))
   369→        };
   370→        command
   371→            .arg(mode.command_name())
   372→            .arg("--json")
   373→            .arg("-n")
   374→            .arg(effective_limit.to_string())
   375→            .arg(query)
   376→            .stdout(Stdio::piped())
   377→            .stderr(Stdio::piped());
   378→
   379→        if matches!(mode, SearchMode::Hybrid { rerank: false }) {
   380→            command.arg("--no-rerank");
   381→        }
   382→
   383→        let output = self.run_with_timeout(command).await?;
   384→        if !output.status.success() {

> AGENT

Pushed. Both PR comments are fixed:

1. **httpd**: Removed the `Other(Box<dyn Error>)` escape hatch — all error sites were already mapped to typed variants
2. **mcp-agent-bridge**: Replaced single-variant enum with `pub type Error = moltis_mcp::error::Error`, dropped `thiserror` dependency

To your question about code-index, chat, tools, and gateway — those 4 already have `thiserror` and use it. They were in the "correct" bucket from the initial audit (both `anyhow` + `thiserror`). Gateway uses thiserror at module level (e.g. `pairing.rs`, `nodes.rs`) rather than a single crate-level Error, but it does have typed errors.
